# Skills · M · part 98

831 entries, metabolite-network-construction to metadata-api. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **metabolite-network-construction**: Use when when you have a list of input metabolites (e.g., from differential metabolomics analysis) and need to: (1) contextualize them within known metabolic pathways; (2) assess their structural importance in the pathway network rather than statistical significance alone; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-network-construction))
- **metabolite-network-covariance-analysis**: Use when you have normalized metabolite abundance data from MetaboAnalyst or similar preprocessing and need to transition from univariate/pairwise correlation analysis to network-level inference that captures conditional dependencies (partial correlations) between metabolites under specific. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-network-covariance-analysis))
- **metabolite-network-database-matching**: Use when the input is a picked-peak set from an INADEQUATE NMR spectrum and the goal is compound annotation by clustering peaks into same-compound networks and matching them against a simulated INADEQUATE metabolite database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-network-database-matching))
- **metabolite-network-diffusion-propagation**: Use when after cluster-based filtering of KEGG candidates has produced a set of candidate metabolites with assigned scores, but before final annotation ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-network-diffusion-propagation))
- **metabolite-network-edge-list-generation**: Use when longitudinal metabolomics data (e.g., NMR-derived concentration matrices over sequential cell-state transitions) must be converted into sparse metabolite networks with per-transition edge lists. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-network-edge-list-generation))
- **metabolite-network-format-conversion**: Use when after downloading GNPS molecular networking results (from GNPS1 or GNPS2 workflows), use this skill to extract and standardize the compressed archive into named, canonicalized files (spectra.mgf, molecular_families.tsv, annotations.tsv, file_mappings.tsv/. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-network-format-conversion) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-network-topology-extraction**: Use when after computing a Jacobian matrix from metabolomics covariance data, when you need to identify and visualize the structure of metabolite interactions (which metabolites regulate or influence which others) and their relative strengths. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-network-topology-extraction))
- **metabolite-node-assignment-onto-pathway-graph**: Use when empirical metabolomics data (time-course metabolite intensities, boundary fluxes, isotope labeling) must be placed onto KEGG-derived pathway nodes of a genome-annotated organism to infer missing or unannotated reactions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-node-assignment-onto-pathway-graph))
- **metabolite-nomenclature-consistency-analysis**: Use when a merged metabolomics dataset has been assembled (e.g. via InChIKey-based MMS) and you need to audit naming consistency, count synonym-hidden merges, and report same/different-name statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-nomenclature-consistency-analysis))
- **metabolite-nomenclature-standardization**: Use when an input metabolomics dataset carries raw or inconsistently formatted metabolite names (e.g. from a Metabolomics Workbench study via getmwstudies) that must be mapped to RefMet standard classes before enrichment scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-nomenclature-standardization))
- **metabolite-origin-classification**: Use when an untargeted metabolomics feature/annotation table needs compound-origin labels (e.g., nutrient, drug, MDM, plant, peptide, halogenated) to prioritize metabolites for pathway analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-origin-classification))
- **metabolite-output-interpretation**: Use when after running CyProduct with a query molecule (SMILES or SDF) and a list of CYP450 enzymes, use this skill to collect, organize, and validate the predicted metabolite results stored in the output folder. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-output-interpretation))
- **metabolite-over-representation-analysis**: Use when you have a list of significant metabolites (e.g. absolute fold change &gt;= 1 and adjusted p-value &lt; 0.05 from differential metabolomics) and need to rank KEGG pathways by enrichment significance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-over-representation-analysis))
- **metabolite-overdispersion-correction**: Use when working with untransformed metabolomics count data (e.g., c57_nos2KO_mouse_countDF) that will be input to variance-sensitive methods such as random forest classification or univariate statistical tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-overdispersion-correction))
- **metabolite-overlap-comparison-across-datasets**: Use when two metabolomics studies of related conditions (e.g., acute infection vs. chronic carriage) each yield significant-metabolite lists, and the goal is to classify shared metabolites by concordant/discordant direction and identify condition-only elevated metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-overlap-comparison-across-datasets))
- **metabolite-pairwise-correlation-computation**: Use when raw metabolite abundance profiles (e.g., tomato pericarp metabolic profiles across biological replicates) must be converted into a nodes-and-edges correlation network for downstream network analysis or pathway prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-pairwise-correlation-computation))
- **metabolite-panel-feature-selection**: Use when a supervised classifier built on the full preprocessed GCxGC-TOFMS metabolite matrix separates known clinical classes (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-panel-feature-selection))
- **metabolite-panel-integration-quantitation**: Use when analyzing standard one-dimensional 1H NMR spectra (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-panel-integration-quantitation))
- **metabolite-panel-quantification**: Use when the input is standard 1D 1H NMR serum/plasma spectra (e.g., Bruker IVDr 600 MHz) and a targeted panel of metabolite concentrations (22 spin systems) is needed without manual peak fitting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-panel-quantification))
- **metabolite-pathway-annotation**: Use when a metabolomics hit list needs biochemical pathway labels or pathway-level enrichment direction (e.g., KEGG sets, NES from single-sample GSEA) to interpret oncoprotein-driven metabolic phenotypes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-pathway-annotation))
- **metabolite-pathway-annotation-mapping**: Use when after peak detection and statistical association or classification analysis has identified a set of significant peaks, use this skill when you need to move from individual feature-level results (peak intensities, p-values, importance scores) to functional biological interpretation via. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-pathway-annotation-mapping) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-pathway-association-analysis**: Use when you have a metabolite-pathway association table (e.g., MetENP's met_path output from a Metabolomics Workbench study such as ST000915) and need to recover the reactions, organism-specific gene IDs, and EC numbers behind each pathway, plus whether each metabolite is a reactant or product. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-pathway-association-analysis))
- **metabolite-pathway-association-mapping**: Use when you have a list of metabolite names or identifiers detected in your samples and need to assign them to known metabolic pathways before computing pathway dysregulation scores, performing pathway-level machine learning, or conducting metabolite-pathway regression analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-pathway-association-mapping))
- **metabolite-pathway-enrichment-analysis**: Use when a preprocessed peak-intensity/concentration matrix has already yielded a shortlist of significantly changed metabolites (via P/FDR/|log2FC| or PLS-DA/OPLS-DA VIP thresholds) and the goal is pathway-level biological interpretation via MSEA and MetPA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-pathway-enrichment-analysis--b4a4fd3929413241))
- **metabolite-pathway-enrichment-analysis**: Use when a cluster-level marker metabolite list (e.g. from spatialmeta.pp.rank_gene_and_metabolite_groups) needs pathway-level interpretation, such as testing whether Imm_4 marker metabolites like Phosphatidylglycerol and PA (24:0/24:1(15Z)) indicate upregulated lipid metabolic pathways. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-pathway-enrichment-analysis--e4e74cf5c2e00fda))
- **metabolite-pathway-mapping**: Use when after metabolite detection and normalization from Metabolomics Workbench format data, when you have a table of metabolite names or identifiers (rows=metabolites, columns=samples) and need to assign pathway membership to compute pathway-level enrichment statistics or visualize. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-pathway-mapping))
- **metabolite-pathway-mapping**: Use when you have a metabolite abundance matrix with metabolite names and need personalized pathway-level features for classification or survival analysis; also when you need a metabolite-pathway lookup table to select a specific pathway (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-pathway-mapping))
- **metabolite-pathway-over-representation-analysis**: Use when you have per-group differential metabolite tables (e.g., male vs female tumors from a massstat differential analysis) and need to know which KEGG/SMPDB biological pathways are specifically enriched in one group but not the other. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-pathway-over-representation-analysis))
- **metabolite-pathway-regression**: Use when you have a PDS pathway-activity matrix and a metabolite-to-pathway mapping table from Lilikoi's feature-mapping/dimension-transformation steps, and you need to attribute a selected pathway feature to its constituent metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-pathway-regression))
- **metabolite-peak-amplitude-quantification**: Use when raw 1D NMR spectra (e.g., spiked urine FIDs at 0-80 uL spike-in volumes) must be deconvolved into peak tables so metabolite peak amplitudes can be correlated with concentration or volume series. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-peak-amplitude-quantification))
- **metabolite-peak-annotation**: Use when after peak detection has identified significant m/z and retention time features in untargeted or targeted mass spectrometry data (as a .raw, .d, or mzXML file). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-peak-annotation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-peak-area-correlation-across-preprocessing-methods**: Use when validating automated MSI-CE-MS preprocessing against a manual analyst workflow by comparing peak areas across metabolites and precision on repeat pooled QC samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-peak-area-correlation-across-preprocessing-methods))
- **metabolite-peak-assignment-from-nmr**: Use when you have a 1D ¹H NMR spectrum (as chemical shift vs. intensity) and a corresponding peak list (chemical shift values), and you need to identify which metabolites are responsible for each detected peak. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-peak-assignment-from-nmr) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-percent-variation-calculation**: Use when an eRah deconvolution/alignment run yields relative concentrations of putatively identified compounds for two sample groups (e.g. HIAE vs CTR) and you need per-metabolite percent variation and rank-sum significance to report dysregulated metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-percent-variation-calculation))
- **metabolite-permeability-classification**: Use when computing exportable metabolites from a draft reconstruction prior to community-dependent gap filling, or when building the leakage-enlarged minimal medium for COMMIT. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-permeability-classification))
- **metabolite-phenotype-association-partial-correlation**: Use when you have a SummarizedExperiment object containing NMR or MS metabolomic data with aligned phenotype information (BMI, disease status, age, gender), and you need to identify metabolites associated with a continuous or categorical outcome while controlling for known confounders that might. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-phenotype-association-partial-correlation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-prediction-comparison-baseline-models**: Use when you have paired microbiome (genus-level 16S or functional profiles) and metabolome datasets (e.g., mass spectrometry or metabolomics panels) with 100+ samples, and you want to demonstrate that a new prediction method outperforms prior work. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-prediction-comparison-baseline-models) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-prediction-correlation-analysis**: Use when after training a neural network or regression model to predict metabolomic profiles from microbiome data. - **metabolite-prediction-cyp450-enzyme-specificity**: Use when you have a small-molecule query (acetaminophen, drug candidate, xenobiotic, or other compound as SMILES or SDF) and need to predict which metabolites would be formed if that molecule were metabolized by a defined subset of the nine major human CYP450 isoforms (1A2, 2A6, 2B6, 2C8, 2C9. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-prediction-cyp450-enzyme-specificity))
- **metabolite-prediction-pathway-selection**: Use when when you have a small-molecule structure (SMILES, MOL, or SDF format) and need to predict its metabolic fate across one or more biological systems. - **metabolite-prediction-threshold-determination**: Use when after training and cross-validating a regression model (e.g., neural network or Elastic Net) that predicts metabolite abundances from microbiome features, you have Spearman correlation coefficients (SCCs) for each metabolite between predicted and observed values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-prediction-threshold-determination))
- **metabolite-predictor-annotation**: Use when you have ranked feature importances (e.g., random forest top predictors) for metabolites and want to link them to known drug targets and evaluate enrichment of synergistic drug combinations against a random background. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-predictor-annotation))
- **metabolite-predictor-overlap-analysis**: Use when you have trained per-metabolite random-forest models with ranked feature importances and want to quantify whether top predictors are enriched for curated SL gene pairs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-predictor-overlap-analysis))
- **metabolite-presence-absence-classification-evaluation**: Use when a trained NMRQNet CRNN model must be scored on held-out simulated nine-metabolite 1H NMR spectra for presence/absence identification accuracy. - **metabolite-profile-missing-value-imputation**: Use when a metabolite concentrations matrix (samples x metabolites, e.g. introgression-line pericarp profiles) contains missing entries that must be filled before computing pairwise Pearson correlations for network construction. - **metabolite-protein-interaction-classification**: Use when gold-standard MPI pairs (piazza, reznik, STITCH ecoli/yeast) are available and features must be computed from constraint-based shadow prices to train and benchmark an MPI classifier. - **metabolite-protein-interaction-data-processing**: Use when a trans-omic interaction table (e.g. BRENDA allosteric metabolite-protein TSV) carries EC numbers in one column but the target pathway layer (KEGG rn map) requires reaction IDs, blocking cross-layer edge attachment. - **metabolite-prototype-bridging**: Use when LC-MS feature tables from non-targeted metabolite identification must be linked back to prototype database entries, especially for TCM herbal components whose metabolic transformations (e.g. among components sharing the same parent nucleus) need bridging. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-pubchemcid-annotation-mapping) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-pvalue-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when metabolite-level p-values (e.g. from two-tailed t-tests per compound) must be converted into a filtered list of differentially abundant metabolites for over-representation analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-pvalue-multiple-testing-correction))
- **metabolite-pvalue-weighted-fisher-combination**: Use when metabolomics studies disclose only overall statistical results (P-value and fold-change, no raw data, error, or variance) and a meta-analysis estimate of compound-level significance is needed. - **metabolite-quality-control-filtering**: Use when after feature extraction (Asari) has produced a full feature table from mzML data, but before normalization and annotation. - **metabolite-quality-metric-assessment**: Use when after drift correction has been applied to a MetaboSet object, and before imputation and batch correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-quality-metric-assessment) · 2 copies)

- **metabolite-ranking-by-annotation-score**: Use when when you have generated a set of candidate metabolites for a given experimental MS/MS spectrum and need to determine which candidate is most likely to be the true metabolite. - **metabolite-ranking-by-network-centrality**: Use when when you have a directed metabolic network (digraph) and want to identify which metabolites are most central to observed perturbations; specifically when you have (1) a global network as an edge list, (2) seed nodes with known or hypothesized perturbation (e. - **metabolite-record-deduplication**: Use when you have extracted metabolite records from two or more public metabolomics databases (e.g., HMDB, MassBank, METLIN) and need to merge them into a single reference database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-record-deduplication))
- **metabolite-reference-library-filtering**: Use when you have defined one or more proton NMR spectral regions-of-interest (ROIs) with lower and upper chemical-shift bounds (in ppm) from an experimental NMR spectrum of a biological sample, and you need to identify which metabolites in a reference database (HMDB) have published 1H NMR shifts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-reference-library-filtering) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-reference-srrm-validation**: Use when preparing or verifying the reference libraries (chemical shifts + SRRMs per metabolite, split by serum/plasma/urine/CSF/saliva/sweat/feces/tissue) that a ROI-based NMR identification program consumes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-reference-srrm-validation))
- **metabolite-retention-time-comparison**: Use when after executing retention-order prediction on a test set of metabolites, you have both predicted retention orderings and experimentally measured ground-truth retention orderings for the same set of compounds, and you need to quantify prediction accuracy to validate the model's performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-retention-time-comparison))
- **metabolite-rt-library-export**: Use when a reviewed metScribeR storage_object.RDS contains manually labeled peaks (good/bad/multimodal) and standards with InChIKeys, and the goal is to produce a final exported_metScribeR_library.csv plus MoNA MS/MS spectra and review figures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-rt-library-export))
- **metabolite-sample-clustering-visualization**: Use when when you have a feature-by-sample metabolomic matrix (finalData) and corresponding sample group labels (finalLabel) and need to visualize how samples cluster together and separate by group using hierarchical clustering; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-sample-clustering-visualization))
- **metabolite-score-projection**: Use when you have Nightingale Health 1H-NMR metabolomics assay output (metabolite concentrations in a samples × features matrix) and you want to compute a published metabolic risk score or surrogate biomarker (mortality risk, metabolic age, cardiovascular event risk, type-2 diabetes risk, COVID-19. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-score-projection) · 4 copies)
- **metabolite-seed-molecule-assignment**: Use when preannotated metabolite candidates (accurate m/z, RT, MS2) from a reaction-based pseudo-MS1 candidature step must be linked back to their parent pesticides via MS2 spectral similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-seed-molecule-assignment))
- **metabolite-selection-result-verification**: Use when an iMAP SVM feature-selection run on the colorectal cancer serum dataset (113 metabolites, 234 samples in CRC/polyp/healthy groups) must be validated against the published 10-metabolite selection before downstream use. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-selection-result-verification))
- **metabolite-set-activity-scoring**: Use when when you have log2-normalized, standardized peak intensity data (rows=peaks, columns=samples) with compound annotations (peak-to-metabolite mappings via KEGG/ChEBI IDs) and need to collapse individual peak signals into pathway-level summary scores for statistical comparison across. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-set-activity-scoring) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-set-activity-scoring**: Use when you have log2-transformed, standardized peak intensity matrices with metabolite annotations (peak ID → KEGG/ChEBI IDs) and need to test whether groups of peaks co-vary systematically within known pathways or spectral groupings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/metabolite-set-activity-scoring))
- **metabolite-set-analysis**: Use when you have a metabolite intensity matrix (rows=metabolites or peaks, columns=samples) paired with metabolite-to-pathway or metabolite-to-feature-group annotations, and you want to score activity levels across pathways or metabolite groupings in a way that tolerates missing peaks and. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-set-analysis) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-set-annotation-mapping**: Use when you have a metabolomics peak intensity matrix with feature IDs (m/z, retention time, or arbitrary peak identifiers) and need to assign these peaks to standardized metabolite databases or spectral groupings (KEGG compounds, ChEBI IDs, GNPS Molecular Families, or MS2LDA Mass2Motifs) before. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-set-annotation-mapping) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-set-composition-validation**: Use when after constructing a background set for ORA in metabolomics: you have loaded an experimental detection list and a metabolomics pathway database, applied background-set construction logic, and need to confirm that the resulting background set has the correct size, composition, pathway. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/metabolite-set-composition-validation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-set-decomposition-plage**: Use when you have peak intensity data from metabolomics experiments with annotated metabolites assigned to known groupings (KEGG pathways, Reactome, GNPS Molecular Families, or MS2LDA Mass2Motifs) and need to identify which metabolite sets change significantly across experimental comparisons while. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-set-decomposition-plage) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have differential metabolomics results (p-values and log2 fold changes) for a set of metabolites and want to identify which known metabolic pathways or metabolite sets show concerted enrichment in your condition of interest. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-set-enrichment-analysis))
- **metabolite-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when analyzing METASPACE-annotated spatial or single-cell metabolomics matrices (MS1-level annotations with isomer/isobar ambiguity) to identify enriched metabolite classes or pathways between conditions, e.g. steatotic vs untreated HepaRG cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-set-enrichment-analysis))
- **metabolite-set-enrichment-benchmarking**: Use when comparing pathway-ranking methods (ORA, mPLAGE, GSEA) for robustness to missing peak annotations, or when reproducing the synthetic decoy-feature benchmark from the PALS paper (DOI 10.3390/metabo11020103). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-set-enrichment-benchmarking))
- **metabolite-set-file-parsing-and-validation**: Use when when a user has prepared a custom collection of metabolite sets (e.g., from spectral fragmentation clustering, literature curation, or domain-specific grouping) in CSV or JSON format and wants to score their activity levels using PALS without modifying the core PALS codebase. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/metabolite-set-file-parsing-and-validation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-set-knowledgebase-interpretation**: Use when you have an LC-MS peak table (m/z features) and optionally MS2-annotated compounds and need to infer which metabolic pathways are significantly changed between conditions, without compound-level identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-set-knowledgebase-interpretation))
- **metabolite-set-overlap-coefficient-computation**: Use when ORA has been run on the same metabolomics dataset against two or more pathway databases and you need a single number capturing how concordant the biological function calls are across databases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-set-overlap-coefficient-computation))
- **metabolite-set-significance-filtering**: Use when a feature table has metabolites grouped into sets beyond curated pathways (GNPS Molecular Families, MS2LDA Mass2Motifs) and the goal is to prioritise which sets change significantly between case and control groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-set-significance-filtering))
- **metabolite-signal-assignment-nmr**: Use when assigning metabolite signals in calibrated 1D 1H NMR spectra of serum/plasma (e.g. Bruker IVDr, 600 MHz) to the 22-metabolite panel via the 24 predefined spin-system bins from SMolESY-select. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-signal-assignment-nmr))
- **metabolite-signal-drift-detection**: Use when when you have multi-batch metabolomics data (SummarizedExperiment object with raw or log-transformed assays) and need to assess whether specific metabolites exhibit systematic signal drift across experimental run order or strong batch effects that would justify hierarchical normalisation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-signal-drift-detection) · 4 copies)
- **metabolite-signal-extraction-from-lc-hrms**: Use when you have raw untargeted LC/HRMS data (mzXML, mzML, or netCDF format) from population-scale studies (n &gt; 500 samples) and need to extract a comprehensive peaklist with aligned features across all samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-signal-extraction-from-lc-hrms) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-significance-categorization**: Use when proton NMR spectra of biological samples (serum, saliva, urine, CSF, tissues) have user-defined ROIs with trend and significance annotations and you need metabolites classified by trend and statistical significance for disease-vs-control comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-significance-categorization))
- **metabolite-significance-filtering-volcano-plot**: Use when you have meta-analyzed metabolomic results from multiple studies with p-values and fold-change estimates (e.g., from amanida quantitative meta-analysis), and you need to identify compounds that meet both statistical rigor (p ≤ 0.05) and biological magnitude (|fold-change| ≥ 3. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-significance-filtering-volcano-plot))
- **metabolite-significance-testing**: Use when you have metabolite names (or names + concentrations) with sample metadata from a Metabolomics Workbench study and need to determine which metabolites are significantly altered between two groups (e.g., Normal vs Cirrhosis) before enrichment analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-significance-testing))
- **metabolite-similarity-scoring**: Use when you have an unknown compound's mass spectrum (m/z peaks and intensities) in . ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-similarity-scoring) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-spatial-coexpression-clustering**: Use when a spatial metabolomics feature matrix (pixel X/Y coordinates x m/z ion intensities, e.g. AFAD-ESI 100 um data) is QC-filtered and the goal is metabolite-level spatial co-expression pattern discovery, not pixel-level region clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-spatial-coexpression-clustering))
- **metabolite-spectral-data-merging**: Use when you have two or more mass spectral libraries in different formats (NIST binary exports converted to MSP, MoNA downloads, RIKEN public databases, GNPS MGF, or batches of in-house standards in separate MSP files) and need to combine them with consistent metadata (SMILES, InChIKey, molecular. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-spectral-data-merging) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-spectral-matching**: Use when you have an experimental mass spectrum (or a set of spectra from LC-MS/MS data) and need to identify the underlying metabolite(s) by comparing against known reference spectra in GNPS or a local indexed repository. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-spectral-matching) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-spectrum-match-scoring**: Use when tandem MS spectra (MGF/mzXML/mzML/mzData) must be searched against a natural product structure database and matches filtered at a target-decoy FDR level, or when reproducing DEREPLICATOR+ compound/MSM counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-spectrum-match-scoring))
- **metabolite-stability-assessment-across-cohorts**: Use when you have uploaded a pre-analytical data table containing sample metadata, processing timestamps (pre- and post-centrifugation), and NMR metabolomic measurements for a cohort of peripheral blood samples (plasma/serum), and you need to quantify the magnitude and direction of metabolite. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-stability-assessment-across-cohorts) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-stability-database-retrieval**: Use when you have measured metabolites or lipids from blood samples (plasma or serum) and need to assess whether quantitative results may be compromised by pre-analytical handling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-stability-database-retrieval))
- **metabolite-stability-thresholding**: Use when a biobank sample cohort has pre- and postcentrifugation timestamps and you need to determine, per metabolic parameter and tube type, the delay at which a 20% concentration change occurs and flag minor vs major changes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-stability-thresholding))
- **metabolite-stability-time-point-extraction**: Use when you have metabolite concentrations measured under controlled pre- or postcentrifugation delays across tube types (EDTA, LiHep, serum) and need per-metabolite 20%-change stability thresholds to QC a biobank cohort. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-stability-time-point-extraction))
- **metabolite-stability-timepoint-analysis**: Use when NMR-quantified plasma/serum metabolite levels from blood samples subjected to controlled pre- and postcentrifugation delays need to be modeled over time, or when a sample cohort's processing-delay distributions must be translated into predicted metabolite alterations (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-stability-timepoint-analysis))
- **metabolite-standard-library-construction**: Use when authentic metabolite standards have been run in positive and negative ESI modes as mzML files and you need to construct an in-house RT/m/z library without collecting new MS/MS spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-standard-library-construction))
- **metabolite-standard-library-quality-control**: Use when building or curating an in-house LC-MS authentic-standard library from mzML files and you must set peak-filtering criteria, manually review peak quality, and check for crossed/unresolvable adducts before library export. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-standard-library-quality-control))
- **metabolite-standard-recovery-evaluation**: Use when a new annotation or feature-grouping tool must be quantitatively validated against standards of known identity and mass, and a per-compound recovery rate is needed. - **metabolite-stoichiometry-computation**: Use when when you have quantified intracellular metabolite abundances (LC-MS normalized values) for multiple cell lines or samples, a metabolic network model with reaction stoichiometry, and you need to predict how substrate availability translates into metabolic flux differences. - **metabolite-structural-annotation-integration**: Use when after statistical analysis (e.g., MB-PLS with permutation testing) has identified a subset of significant LC-MS features (p &lt; 0.05 or similar threshold) that require structural interpretation. - **metabolite-structural-class-pair-categorization**: Use when a SIMILE/cosine spectral-alignment results table must be labeled by metabolite structural class (flavonoid vs. indole, etc.) and uniquely-SIMILE pairs need MCS-category annotation and mass-delta interpretation. - **metabolite-structural-network-construction**: Use when after MamsiStructSearch has completed structural clustering of statistically significant LC-MS features (p &lt; 0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-structural-network-construction) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-structure-annotation**: Use when an LC/MS feature table contains enriched features (e.g. CA-dependent metabolites enriched in hacl-1(tm6725)) and you need to collapse CAMERA feature groups, predict formulas, and confirm tentative structures from MS2 scans. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-structure-annotation))
- **metabolite-structure-annotation**: Use when you have chemical structures (as SMILES or molecular structure formats) of environmental compounds and need to predict their microbial metabolites and degradation pathways. - **metabolite-structure-annotation-integration**: Use when you have a set of candidate transformed structures generated by biotransformation rules (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-structure-annotation-integration) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-structure-atom-numbering**: Use when manually building a PathModel knowledge base (.lp file) that requires metabolite structures to be represented as ASP facts with atoms and bonds so reactions can be compared at the atom level. - **metabolite-structure-database-matching**: Use when a metabolomics mass feature list must be annotated with candidate structures by enumerating adducts (default ~40) and matching neutral masses against a structure database within a 20 ppm tolerance. - **metabolite-structure-format-conversion**: Use when when importing candidate metabolite structures from public chemical databases (PubChem, ChEBI, etc.) for use in MAGMa-based annotation workflows, or when integrating external structure datasets that may use divergent molecular representation formats or contain non-standard chemical. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-structure-format-conversion) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-structure-generation**: Use when CypBoM has flagged reactive bonds (〈X, Y〉 pairs with p ≥ τ = 1/(β+1)) for a substrate–CYP450 isoform pair and you must convert those BoM predictions into concrete metabolite structures with per-metabolite scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-structure-generation))
- **metabolite-structure-generation**: Use when you have a small-molecule structure (SMILES, MOL, or SDF format) and need to predict its metabolic fate in a specific biological context (mammalian phase I/II metabolism, human gut microbiota, or soil/aquatic microbial degradation). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-structure-generation))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-structure-matching-against-reference-sets))
- **metabolite-structure-prediction**: Use when you have a parent compound (or set of compounds) in SMILES, MOL, or SDF format and need to predict plausible metabolite structures and pathways in a specific biological context (mammalian Phase I/II metabolism, human gut microbiota, or soil/aquatic microbial degradation). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-structure-prediction) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-subnetwork-extraction**: Use when analyzing high-dimensional longitudinal metabolomics concentration tables (e.g., 1H-NMR profiles of sequential cell populations) and you need to identify which metabolites and covariance relationships define a specific biological transition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-subnetwork-extraction))
- **metabolite-superclass-stratified-sampling**: Use when benchmarking in silico spectral prediction or identification tools across metabolomics domains (exposomics, foodomics, human metabolomics) and you need unbiased, domain-representative test sets drawn from a larger spectral database. - **metabolite-synonym-reconciliation**: Use when two metabolite annotation engines (e.g. SpaMTP and METASPACE/pySM) annotate overlapping m/z peaks from the same spatial metabolomics dataset and synonym naming differences must be reconciled before computing per-database match percentages. - **metabolite-synonym-resolution**: Use when a metabolomics meta-analysis input table mixes identifier types (compound synonyms, InChIKeys, SMILES) so that the same compound appears under multiple labels and would otherwise inflate unique-ID counts and deflate vote counts. - **metabolite-tandem-ms-library-curation**: Use when you have multiple tandem MS/MS libraries in different formats (msp, mgf) from different providers (NIST, RIKEN, MoNA, GNPS) with incomplete or inconsistent structural annotations (missing SMILES or molecular formula fields) and need to combine them into unified, polarity-specific msp files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-tandem-ms-library-curation) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-target-definition**: Use when building a targeted LC-MS assay in TarMet and you must define target metabolite m/z windows from a molecular formula/adduct, or derive isotopologue m/z series for a stable-isotope tracer with a chosen isotope and max isotopologues N. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-target-definition))
- **metabolite-target-list-formatting**: Use when you have a raw LC–MS compound metadata file (xlsx or csv) with heterogeneous column names and column order, and you need to prepare it for targeted peak detection in TARDIS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-target-list-formatting) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-taxonomy-database-lookup**: Use when when you have MS/MS-annotated features from a natural extract (via SIRIUS, CANOPUS, or ISDB) and need to compute the Literature Component or Class Component of a priority rank—i. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-taxonomy-database-lookup) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-to-gene-mapping**: Use when you have metabolomic data (e.g., from LC-MS or GC-MS comparing patient to controls) showing differential abundant metabolites (DAMs), candidate genes from exome sequencing or variant calling, and access to a protein–protein or gene–gene interaction network (e.g., STRING). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-to-gene-mapping) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-to-pathway-mapping**: Use when you have a peak intensity matrix (samples × metabolites) with assigned metabolite annotations (peak ID → KEGG or ChEBI compound ID), and you need to link those identities to known metabolic pathways, molecular families, or mass2motifs before computing pathway activity scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-to-pathway-mapping))
- **metabolite-to-som-annotation-mapping**: Use when comparing a metabolite/Byproduct-predicting tool against SoM-only predictors on a common benchmark, so predicted products must be converted to atom-level SoM labels (e.g., TP/(TP+FN+FP) Jaccard over CYP450 isoforms). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-to-som-annotation-mapping))
- **metabolite-trait-gwas-correction**: Use when running mGWAS of a pan-genome orthogene matrix against a large numeric phenotypic dataset (e.g. metabolomics traits per strain) and you must control false positives with trait-aware correction before nominating candidate genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/metabolite-trait-gwas-correction))
- **metabolite-trend-classification**: Use when when you have compiled p-values and fold-changes from multiple metabolomics studies and need to assign a single trend label (up/down/none) to each metabolite for meta-analysis or cross-study comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-trend-classification))
- **metabolite-trend-classification-updown-regulation**: Use when input data consist only of compound identifiers and trend/behaviour labels (up-regulated, down-regulated, or no trend) per study — i.e. studies that do not disclose p-values, fold-changes, or standard deviations — and a qualitative per-compound meta-analysis is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-trend-classification-updown-regulation))
- **metabolite-univariate-statistical-testing**: Use when a processed LC-MS feature table (samples x metabolite features with group labels) must be tested feature-by-feature for differential abundance between two classes, producing fold change, p-value, and adjusted p-value per feature for volcano plots. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-univariate-statistical-testing))
- **metabolite-variance-boxplot-visualization**: Use when you have a metabolomics matrix and an lmm2met fit object and need to visually validate that linear mixed-effects modelling reduced per-metabolite variance, as in Figure S-1/Fig. 3a. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-variance-boxplot-visualization))
- **metabolite-vote-counting-qualitative-analysis**: Use when you have metabolomic results from multiple studies with only compound identifiers, directional trend labels (up-regulated, down-regulated, or no trend), and bibliographic references — and you want to quantify agreement on metabolite behavior direction across studies without requiring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-vote-counting-qualitative-analysis))
- **metabolite-wise-pareto-scaling**: Use when preprocessing urine metabolite intensity tables (e.g. synthetic_metabolomics.csv) for downstream integration, where metabolites differ in variance and must be scaled before MOFA or similarity network analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-wise-pareto-scaling))
- **metabolites-assignment-table-construction**: Use when the goal is to convert a cohort of raw 1D 1H NMR serum/plasma spectra (e.g. Bruker IVDr 600 MHz) into an automated, per-sample quantitative table for a predefined panel of ~22 abundant metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolites-assignment-table-construction))
- **metabolization-review**: Use this skill when a user asks whether a repeated capability, workflow, tool, habit, or agent behavior has become substrate through settlement, repetition, time, stability, and lower attention burden. ([GodSpeedAI/GodSpeed-Agent](https://github.com/GodSpeedAI/GodSpeed-Agent/tree/HEAD/skills/metabolization-review))
- **metabolizing-knowledge**: Use when asked whether something delivered earlier still holds, when closing a milestone or running a periodic sweep over what was already marked verified, or when the set of live claims has grown too large to read and dead entries need retiring. DO NOT invoke to add, audit, or retire a decision record — route that to ledger:curating-decision-records. DO NOT invoke to propose removing code — route that to ledger:proving-code-is-dead. ([qbs784/ledger](https://github.com/qbs784/ledger/tree/HEAD/skills/metabolizing-knowledge))
- **metabologenomic-correlation-scoring**: Use when a strain collection has both genome-mined BGCs (with iPRESTO sub-cluster motifs or SCCs) and MS/MS data with Mass2Motifs, and the goal is to link molecular substructures to their biosynthetic sub-clusters via co-occurrence correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabologenomic-correlation-scoring))
- **metabologenomic-database-construction**: Use when you have genome FASTA or annotated genome files (antiSMASH .gbk, BOA .annotated.txt) and wish to discover ribosomally synthesized and post-translationally modified peptides (RiPPs) by integrating genomic and mass spectrometry data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabologenomic-database-construction) · 3 copies)
- **metabolome-genome-link-tabulation**: Use when you have downloaded the PoDP Zenodo JSON deposit and need to reproduce or audit the published dataset statistics (Box 1) — e.g. validating that 1,306 metagenomes, 1,268 genomes, and 42 MAGs are present. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolome-genome-link-tabulation))
- **metabolome-informed-phenotype-prediction**: Use when a labeled metabolomics feature table (samples × metabolite intensities with phenotype metadata, e.g. age, sex, region) is available and the goal is an automated, metabolome-informed predictive classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolome-informed-phenotype-prediction))
- **metabolome-module-feature-aggregation**: Use when you have MiMeNet biclustered microbe/metabolite modules and WGCNA modules for paired microbiome-metabolome data (e.g. IBD PRISM) and want to determine if module-level features match original features for predicting disease labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolome-module-feature-aggregation))
- **metabolome-wide-association-analysis**: Use when a SummarizedExperiment of metabolic feature intensities (e.g. plasma 1H NMR) plus clinical metadata (phenotype + confounders) must be screened feature-by-feature for phenotype associations with multiple-testing control. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolome-wide-association-analysis))
- **metabolomic-biomarker-pathway-association**: Use when after marker identification (via fold-change, PLS-DA, t-test, or other feature selection methods) has produced a ranked list of metabolic features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-biomarker-pathway-association))
- **metabolomic-data-format-standardization**: Use when you have raw peak table data from liquid chromatography–mass spectrometry (LC-MS) or related metabolomic instruments, generated by one of 12 supported software tools (or already in NOREVA's standardized format), and need to prepare it for preprocessing method evaluation or biomarker. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-data-format-standardization) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomic-data-normalization-post-integration**: Use when immediately after integrating multiple metabolomic datasets from different analytical experiments into a unified feature-by-sample matrix. Batch effects manifest as systematic unwanted variations correlated with analytical batch rather than biological sample groups; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-data-normalization-post-integration))
- **metabolomic-data-preprocessing-optimization**: Use when you have multi-class or time-course metabolomic peak tables (with or without quality control samples and/or internal standards) and need to select the preprocessing workflow from hundreds of candidate combinations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-data-preprocessing-optimization))
- **metabolomic-data-quality-assessment**: Use when when you have raw metabolomics data with missing values in metabolite columns and need to prepare the dataset for downstream statistical analysis (linear mixed models, ANOVA, dimensionality reduction, or prediction models). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-data-quality-assessment))
- **metabolomic-data-structure-formatting**: Use when after peak detection in MZmine2 has produced an MGF file (containing MS1 and MS2 spectra) and a feature abundance table (CSV or BIOM), but before running q2-qemistree tree construction or any QIIME 2-based metabolomic analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-data-structure-formatting) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomic-data-subsetting-by-phenotype**: Use when you have a preprocessed MultiAssayExperiment with metabolite measurements across multiple phenotypic or disease subgroups, and you want to isolate one or more groups for comparison—for example, to compare AKT1-high versus MYC-high prostate tumor samples before applying diff_test for. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-data-subsetting-by-phenotype))
- **metabolomic-data-transformation-and-normalization**: Use when a metabolomic peak table has passed filtering/imputation (S1) and QCS correction (S2) and must be nonlinearly transformed (e.g., LOG) and normalized (e.g., VAS-LIN) before performance assessment in NOREVA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomic-data-transformation-and-normalization))
- **metabolomic-dataset-preprocessing-and-normalization**: Use when you have raw or minimally processed FT-ICR MS peak tables in Formularity . - **metabolomic-edge-list-import-processing**: Use when you have edge-list files of putative biochemical transformations from FT-ICR MS peak mass differences and need a Cytoscape network plus network statistics tables and plots. - **metabolomic-enrichment-score-scaling**: Use when you have per-patient DAM lists (absolute z-score &gt;= 2) from XCMS untargeted LC-MS output and need continuous [0,1] per-gene seed labels for metPropagate label propagation over STRING. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomic-enrichment-score-scaling))
- **metabolomic-feature-alignment**: Use when you have two or more CSV feature tables from independent metabolomic experiments (each with RT, m/z, intensity, isotope, and adduct columns), and you need to merge them into a single aligned feature matrix for downstream batch effect removal, marker identification, or pathway analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-feature-alignment) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomic-feature-count-comparison**: Use when evaluating a hybrid DDA/DIA acquisition strategy against conventional DDA and DIA(SWATH) runs and you need reproducible feature/MS2-count tables with recomputed relative gains. - **metabolomic-feature-extraction-from-mzml**: Use when you have centroid mzML files from LC-MS acquisitions and need to detect, group, and quantify metabolomic features for a PCPFM experiment. - **metabolomic-feature-filtering-threshold-application**: Use when after running the Marr() function on preprocessed metabolomic data (with missing value imputation and normalization already complete) when you need to distinguish reproducible from non-reproducible features and sample pairs. - **metabolomic-feature-imputation**: Use when when a preprocessed metabolomic feature table (e.g., MS-Dial output) retains features and samples that passed filtering for missingness thresholds and m/z validity, but still contain scattered missing values (NA). This skill is appropriate after sample-level filtering (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-feature-imputation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomic-feature-matching**: Use when you have two LC-MS feature tables (each with m/z, retention time, and intensity columns) from separate metabolomic experiments or replicates, and you need to establish which features in dataset A correspond to which features in dataset B to enable comparative or longitudinal analysis. - **metabolomic-feature-recovery-quantification**: Use when comparing LC-MS acquisition workflows (DDA vs DIA vs hybrid DaDIA) and you need to measure how many DDA-derived metabolic features are recovered in the combined data and assess their peak-shape reliability. - **metabolomic-feature-retention-statistics**: Use when after applying the CV_ratio() filtering function to a normalized metabolomic feature matrix (e.g., Urine_RP_NEG_norm.txt) in margheRita, generate retention statistics to report how many features passed the threshold (CV ratio &gt; 1.0) and characterize the distribution of retained CV ratios. - **metabolomic-feature-selection-by-metadata**: Use when a quality-controlled metabolomic feature table plus sample metadata (e.g., age group, sex, brain region) is available and the goal is metadata-driven feature selection and phenotype prediction. - **metabolomic-feature-significance-filtering**: Use when you have a paired-design LC-MS metabolomic feature table (e.g. from XCMS centWave on DDA or DaDIA data) and need to identify statistically significant metabolic features for downstream workflow comparison or metabolite annotation. - **metabolomic-feature-statistical-hypothesis-testing**: Use when when you have a normalized and batch-corrected feature abundance matrix from non-targeted LC-MS/MS metabolomics (e. - **metabolomic-feature-table-assembly**: Use when when you have LC-MS data (mzML or netCDF format) and a pre-defined list of target metabolites (m/z, retention time, and identifiers) that you wish to extract and quantify across multiple samples, rather than performing untargeted feature discovery. - **metabolomic-feature-table-filtering**: Use when after feature detection (e.g., Asari processing of mzML files to feature tables) but before normalization, batch correction, or annotation. - **metabolomic-feature-table-imputation**: Use when after feature detection and peak alignment have produced a feature table with zero or missing values across samples. - **metabolomic-feature-table-processing**: Use when you have a metabolomics feature table (rows=features, columns=samples) generated from LC-MS or GC-MS preprocessing and need to identify which features contain systematic errors from peak integration or alignment. - **metabolomic-feature-tree-construction**: Use when when you have preprocessed LC-MS/MS data (MGF file with MS1 and MS2 spectra and a feature abundance table from MZmine2 or similar peak detection tool) and need to perform chemical phylogeny-based diversity analyses or meta-analyses comparing metabolomic profiles across multiple samples or. - **metabolomic-frailty-subtypes**: Tier B lookup (human): what the paper reports, or simple arithmetic on values you bring; not the paper's model run on one person. - **metabolomic-network-inference**: Use when metabolomic intensity data (rows/columns labelled with KEGG compound IDs such as 'C00042') must be converted into an inferred metabolite interaction network with topology statistics (nodes, edges, neighbours, path length, clustering coefficient, density). - **metabolomic-output-format-validation**: Use when a DaDIA.R run (XCMS 3.11.4+) has produced metabolite_intensity_table.csv and ms2_spectra.mgf and you must verify MGF parsability, required CSV columns, and DDA/DIA source tagging before MS2 spectral matching. - **metabolomic-peak-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when you have a raw or minimally processed metabolomic peak matrix (in standardized or tool-generated format) from multi-class samples with optional quality control (QC) samples and/or internal standards (IS), and you need to generate a single normalized peak table suitable for statistical. - **metabolomic-peak-quality-assessment**: Use when after peak detection in untargeted LC/HRMS workflows, when you have a list of candidate peaks with signal intensity profiles and need to filter them according to data quality thresholds (signal-to-noise ratio, peak width, baseline separation, and isotopic pairing consistency) before. - **metabolomic-peak-table-processing-workflow-execution**: Use when you have metabolomic peak tables (e.g., MetaboLights MTBLS6, MTBLS583, MTBLS403, MTBLS370, MTBLS59, MTBLS656, MTBLS319, MTBLS1569, MTBLS665) and must run a specific method combination across the four processing stages to produce a processed output for performance assessment. - **metabolomic-peak-table-standardization**: Use when a raw or tool-generated metabolomic peak table (e.g., from MetaboLights MTBLS403, MTBLS6, MTBLS656) must be reshaped to NOREVA's unified format before running the R-based workflow scan and ranking. - **metabolomic-qc-feature-filtering**: Use when a metabonomic SummarizedExperiment (metabo_SE) and its per-feature CV table (metabo_CV) need filtering of non-reproducible metabolic features prior to downstream MWAS or STOCSY analysis. - **metabolomic-signal-quantification**: Use when you have raw untargeted LC/MS data in open mzML or mzXML format and need to extract a quantified feature matrix (m/z and retention time coordinates with sample intensities) without prior knowledge of optimal signal detection parameters, batch effects, or quality control samples. - **metabolomic-spectral-annotation-and-molecular-family-clustering**: Use when when you have raw or GNPS-processed MS2 spectral data from microbial strains and need to organize spectra into molecular families (grouped by spectral similarity) while preserving strain provenance, as a prerequisite for linking metabolomic families to gene cluster families (GCFs) via. - **metabolomic-workflow-combinatorial-enumeration**: Use when a metabolomic peak table (case-control, multiclass, or time-course) must be exhaustively scanned across all valid processing method combinations to find the study-specific optimal workflow before S5 multi-criteria assessment. - **metabolomic-workflow-ranking-visualization**: Use when after running NOREVA's multi-class or time-course assessment functions (normulticlassqcall, normulticlassnoall, normulticlassisall, nortimecourseqcall, or nortimecoursenoall) that produce an overall ranking CSV file of preprocessed workflows. - **metabolomic-workflow-scan-optimization**: Use when a raw or unnormalized metabolomic peak table (case-control, multi-class, or time-course, e.g. MetaboLights MTBLS403) needs an objectively optimal combination of filtering, imputation, QCS correction, transformation, and normalization methods. - **metabolomics-classifier-training**: Use when a metabolomics or proteomics feature table with class labels needs supervised classification models whose accuracy must be measured on held-out test samples rather than on the full data set. - **metabolomics-classifier-training**: Use when you have a preprocessed metabolomics feature matrix (expression matrix with metabolite abundances as columns and samples as rows) with corresponding binary or multi-class sample labels, and you need to train and compare classifier performance to select the -performing model for disease. - **metabolomics-collection-router**: Use when an agent needs to find and apply a computational-metabolomics / LC-MS-MS skill from this collection, and optionally ground it against the source paper via Perspicacité before acting. - **metabolomics-compound-confidence-classification**: Use when a batch-corrected targeted metabolomics peak-integrated table must be screened per compound to decide which metabolites are reliable enough to report, flagging those with poor QC reproducibility or high blank background before downstream statistics. - **metabolomics-concentration-data-transformation**: Use when a post-normalization metabolite concentration table (.csv) is right-skewed or spans a wide dynamic range and an element-wise Log10, Log2, square root, or cube root transform is needed before scaling and PCA-based data-treatment assessment. - **metabolomics-condition-wise-count-aggregation**: Use when an XCMS peak table from stable-isotope (13C) labeling experiments has been processed with PuInc_seeker and basepeak_finder and you need to count how many features show isotopic-distribution shifts per condition. - **metabolomics-confounder-adjustment**: Use when a raw metabolomics peak-intensity table (e.g., TEDDY Metabolomics Workbench ST001386) will feed network estimation or differential analysis, and sample covariates like age and sex must be removed from metabolite abundances first. - **metabolomics-contrast-group-comparison**: Use when you have a preprocessed LC-MS/MS feature quantification table with k-medoids cluster assignments from spectral similarity grouping and need feature-set level differential testing between two treatment groups. - **metabolomics-correlation-network-construction**: Use when building metabolite-metabolite association networks from peak-intensity tables, or when explaining why marginal-correlation networks are denser than partial-correlation networks on the same data. - **metabolomics-count-tabulation**: Use when two or more annotation strategies have already assigned results to the same MS/MS peak list (e.g., motif probability memberships and Mass Bank/NIST library hits) and you need comparable coverage counts, per-mode percentages, and a yield ratio.
