([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-peak-mz-matching))
- **metabolomics-peak-table-loading**: Use when you have raw peak tables exported from a tandem mass spectrometry preprocessing tool (e.g. Progenesis, MS-DIAL, or Bruker Metaboscape) and need to integrate them with sample metadata for reproducibility filtering, mispicked-ion removal, or group-based feature exclusion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-peak-table-loading) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-peak-table-normalization-comparison**: Use when a raw QC-annotated GC-MS peak-intensity table (e.g., .xlsx with pool QC and external validation QC samples) must be normalized and the choice among sum-based, SERRF, and SERDA normalization needs empirical benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-peak-table-normalization-comparison))
- **metabolomics-phenotype-association-testing**: Use when an aligned peak-intensity matrix from an untargeted LC/HRMS study (e.g. IDSL.IPA or MS-DIAL output for 499 MTBLS1684 samples) is joined to sample-level phenotype metadata and you need to rank peaks by monotonic association strength with a significance decision at adjusted p &lt; 0.05. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-phenotype-association-testing))
- **metabolomics-pipeline**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia/tree/HEAD/Skills/Workflow_Management/workflows/metabolomics-pipeline) · ★7)
- **metabolomics-pipeline-execution-and-verification**: Use when reproducing a published NMR metabolomics result from a deposited INADEQUATE and/or 13C-JRES spectrum of a labeled sample, where the claim is a concrete per-network count (e.g. 65 of 67 networks with JRES peaks) that must be re-derived. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-pipeline-execution-and-verification))
- **metabolomics-pipeline-report-generation**: Use when a completed maplet pipeline container (a SummarizedExperiment-derived object) must be turned into a reproducible report enumerating every executed function, its arguments, and its plots/statistics tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-pipeline-report-generation))
- **metabolomics-preannotation-json-export**: Use when you have a preprocessed feature table (feature ID, m/z, rtime, intensities) from LC-MS untargeted metabolomics or isotope tracing and need low-level annotation into empirical compounds with inferred neutral masses before database search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-preannotation-json-export))
- **metabolomics-preprocessing-and-covariate-residualization**: Use when raw metabolite intensity data (e.g., Metabolon platform output from Metabolomics Workbench) must be cleaned, batch/run-day normalized, imputed, and converted to covariate-adjusted partial residuals before kernel machine or pathway association tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-preprocessing-and-covariate-residualization))
- **metabolomics-preprocessing-normalization**: Use when you have acquired raw SIMS (secondary ion mass spectrometry) metabolite images aligned with tissue regions and segmented single-cell masks, and you need to extract normalized metabolite intensity values per cell before performing cell-type assignment, VAE embedding, or protein–metabolite. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-preprocessing-normalization) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-preprocessing-parameter-tuning**: Use when configuring or optimizing PeakMeister preprocessing of targeted full-scan MSI-CE-MS mzML files and benchmarking parameter choices against manual vendor-software peak picking and integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-preprocessing-parameter-tuning))
- **metabolomics-preprocessing-pipeline-benchmarking**: Use when comparing missing-value imputation and normalization pipelines for MS-Metabolomics replicate data, or when assessing whether technical vs biological replicate layers differ in metabolite reproducibility via MaRR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-preprocessing-pipeline-benchmarking))
- **metabolomics-preprocessing-starter**: Use this starter when a task lands in the `Metabolomics preprocessing` frontier leaf and the repository has curated resources but no dedicated runtime implementation yet. ([ma-compbio-lab/SkillFoundry](https://github.com/ma-compbio-lab/SkillFoundry/tree/HEAD/skills/metabolomics-and-other-omics/metabolomics-preprocessing-starter) · ★46)
- **metabolomics-principal-component-analysis**: Use when starting a differential metabolomic analysis and you need an unsupervised, label-aware overview of whether sample groups separate metabolome-wide, before committing to hypothesis tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-principal-component-analysis))
- **metabolomics-project-batch-import**: Use when Thermo .raw or SCIEX .wiff files have been converted to Aird format and must be loaded into MetaPro as organized projects/batches before library assignment and Target Search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-project-batch-import))
- **metabolomics-project-construction**: Use when raw metabolite accumulation tables (e.g. GC-MS data for mutants) and their condition metadata must be imported into MetaOmGraph, linked, and saved as a shareable .mog project. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-project-construction))
- **metabolomics-proteomics-context**: Integrate public metabolite, reaction, protein, proteomics, and study evidence. Use for pathway mechanism, biomarker context, multi-omics follow-up, or dataset selection. ([eightmm/codex-science](https://github.com/eightmm/codex-science/tree/HEAD/authored-skills/metabolomics-proteomics-context) · ★4)
- **metabolomics-qc-architecture-specification**: Use when you need to specify, reproduce, or modify the internal control flow of QC4Metabolomics — e.g. mapping how module_Files detects new files, how modules hand off through mariaDB, and how the Shiny dashboard displays quality metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-qc-architecture-specification))
- **metabolomics-qc-based-feature-retention**: Use when a tab-delimited feature abundance table (e.g. MS-DIAL output) has matched sample annotations with class labels distinguishing QC from biological samples, and low-quality features must be filtered before imputation, normalization, or statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-qc-based-feature-retention))
- **metabolomics-qc-data-visualization**: Use when monitoring untargeted MS-based metabolomics acquisitions — e.g. pooled QC injections every 5–10 injections — and you need to visualize RT/m/z deviation, intensity drift, or contaminant levels per batch to catch analytical problems. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-qc-data-visualization))
- **metabolomics-qc-feature-table-handling**: Use when raw peak-picked feature tables from two LC–MS metabolomics datasets must be QC-filtered and summarized (RT, m/z, FI medians) before feature correspondence matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-qc-feature-table-handling))
- **metabolomics-qc-outlier-flagging**: Use when a pooled-QC-injected targeted metabolomics/lipidomics sequence (Sciex MultiQuant txt exports) needs outlying samples flagged before statistical analysis, and you need defensible, reportable outlier criteria based on PCA scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-qc-outlier-flagging))
- **metabolomics-qc-pipeline-execution**: Use when raw LC–MS injections (mzML from Waters RAW converted with msconvert) plus a batch sequence mapping SSS and pooled/intrastudy QC injections must be assessed for platform conditioning and system suitability with TidyMS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-qc-pipeline-execution))
- **metabolomics-qc-rsd-calculation**: Use when a metabolomics/lipidomics sequence contains repeated pooled QC (QCpool) injections and you need to quantitatively assess overall data quality in terms of precision and accuracy per chemical descriptor before reporting the study. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-qc-rsd-calculation))
- **metabolomics-qc-sample-clustering**: Use when a post-data-curation LC–MS feature matrix (e.g. from MetaboLights MTBLS1919) with pooled/intrastudy QC injections is available and you need to check QC clustering, sample outliers, or drift-correlated variance via PCA scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-qc-sample-clustering))
- **metabolomics-qc-summary-extraction**: Use when you have run metaboprep quality_control() on a metabolomics dataset (e.g. Metabolon UPLC-MS/MS or Nightingale NMR) and need the per-cohort sample/metabolite exclusion counts and the filtered dataset exported for reporting or verification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-qc-summary-extraction))
- **metabolomics-qc-threshold-configuration**: Use when a raw metabolomics feature table (e.g. Nightingale NMR, Metabolon xlsx, Olink txt, SomaLogic adat) must be quality-controlled and filtered with explicit, reproducible sample/feature exclusion thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-qc-threshold-configuration))
- **metabolomics-qc-workflow-design**: Use when you must evaluate or correct time-related signal drift in untargeted LC–MS batches that include pooled/intrastudy QC samples acquired in order (e.g., mzML runs from MetaboLights MTBLS1919). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-qc-workflow-design))
- **metabolomics-qc-workflow-implementation**: Use when processing pooled-QC (QCpool)-injected metabolomics or lipidomics datasets — e.g., Sciex MultiQuant .txt exports — that require a standardized, reportable QC workflow before statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-qc-workflow-implementation))
- **metabolomics-qcpool-series-monitoring**: Use when a targeted metabolomics or lipidomics study has pooled QC samples (QCpool) measured at regular intervals across one or more sequences, and you need to detect signal drift, background/carryover effects or out-of-control injections before downstream missing-value handling and outlier removal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-qcpool-series-monitoring))
- **metabolomics-quality-control-boxplot-visualization**: Use when a MetaboDiff MultiAssayExperiment object (Metabolon®-style metabolite measurements) has been imputed and vsn-normalized and you must visually verify near-constant variance and comparable per-sample distributions before differential analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-quality-control-boxplot-visualization))
- **metabolomics-quality-control-cv-analysis**: Use when a 1H-NMR or metabonomic dataset contains QC replicate samples and you must quantify analytical reproducibility per metabolic feature, or decide which features to retain via CV &lt; 0.30 / CV &lt; 0.15 filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-quality-control-cv-analysis))
- **metabolomics-quality-control-filtering**: Use when raw platform-exported metabolomics data (e.g. Metabolon OrigScale xlsx, Nightingale NMR, Olink, SomaLogic adat) must be QC'd, filtered on missingness/outlier thresholds, and summarized before association analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-quality-control-filtering))
- **metabolomics-quality-control-monitoring**: Use when untargeted MS-based metabolomics files (mzML, mzXML, mzData) are being acquired or already acquired and you need to monitor QC parameters such as m/z deviation, retention time deviation, intensity drift, calibration offset, or contaminant levels in (near) real time or retrospectively. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-quality-control-monitoring))
- **metabolomics-quality-control-report-generation**: Use when after completing outlier detection, batch correction, and quality metric calculation on a SummarizedExperiment object using mzQuality's doAnalysis function, and after manually or automatically filtering compounds and samples using the 'use' column in rowData and colData. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-quality-control-report-generation) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-quality-correction-workflow**: Use when an Asari preferred_Feature_table.tsv from a pcpfm experiment shows per-sample TIC differences driven by feature counts, or samples span multiple batches, and the table must be normalized/batch-corrected before downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-quality-correction-workflow))
- **metabolomics-quality-metric-interpretation**: Use when after batch correction of metabolomics data using pooled study quality control (SQC) samples and calculation of compound/internal standard ratios, when you need to decide which compounds are reliable for reporting and which internal standard minimizes technical variation (RSDQC) for each. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/metabolomics-quality-metric-interpretation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-quality-parameter-extraction**: Use when acquired or archived untargeted MS metabolomics files (mzML, mzXML, mzData) must be summarized into per-file quality metrics — RT and m/z deviations, intensity drift, peak shape — for real-time or retrospective QC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-quality-parameter-extraction))
- **metabolomics-quantification**: Load when imputing missing values (min / median / KNN) and normalising (TIC / median / log) a feature × sample metabolomics CSV. - **metabolomics-quantification-report-processing**: Use when a MetaPro run-1 quantification export (.xlsx) is available for a benchmark mixture design with known SB:SA dilution ratios (1/16 to 16/1) and you need to verify that observed log2 fold changes track the designed values before manual peak inspection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-quantification-report-processing))
- **metabolomics-quantification-table-processing**: Use when you have a quantification table (rows = metabolite features, columns = samples with abundance values), corresponding metadata table (sample annotations, groupings), and spectral data files, and you need to produce a unified JSON dashboard artifact that can be loaded into an interactive. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-quantification-table-processing) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-quantitative-results-extraction**: Use when a source card asks to reproduce reported metabolomics counts (peaks/identifications per ionisation mode) from a paper's Results text and supplementary figures, and the deliverable is a validated summary table rather than re-processed spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-quantitative-results-extraction))
- **metabolomics-raw-data-preprocessing**: Use when raw targeted LC–MS runs from public repositories (e.g., MetaboLights MTBLS8735, potentially from different vendors) must be integrated against a target list and QC-clustered PCA is the exploratory endpoint. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-raw-data-preprocessing))
- **metabolomics-raw-data-reprocessing**: Use when raw LC–MS mzML files (e.g. from MetaboLights MTBLS1919) must be converted into a curated, QC-controlled feature matrix for untargeted metabolomics, rather than analyzed with manual or GUI tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-raw-data-reprocessing))
- **metabolomics-raw-data-retrieval-from-repository**: Use when a metabolomics analysis must start from publicly deposited raw spectra — e.g., reproducing a SWATH-DIA benchmark that requires the raw mzML files of a Metabolomics Workbench study plus its MS1 feature table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-raw-data-retrieval-from-repository))
- **metabolomics-regression-modelling**: Use when a metabolomics intensity table plus sample metadata and a model formula require per-metabolite regression — fixed-effects lm, longitudinal mixed-effects (lmer), or Tobit for censored detection-limit values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-regression-modelling))
- **metabolomics-replicate-precision-assessment**: Use when you have an aligned feature table from JPA's peak.alignment step with replicate sample-intensity columns and need to quantify analytical precision of peak-picked (PP) versus MS2-recognized (MR) features before statistical testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-replicate-precision-assessment))
- **metabolomics-replicate-qc-evaluation**: Use when raw LC-MS spectra from a serial dilution series (known dilution design, replicate tubes) are available and you need to validate that a spectra-processing workflow quantifies features proportionally to concentration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-replicate-qc-evaluation))
- **metabolomics-replicate-reproducibility-quantification**: Use when high-dimensional metabolomics (or similar omics) data contain technical or biological replicate samples and you need to identify reproducible metabolites/sample pairs and compare replicate layers, e.g. Tech vs BioTech vs Bio COPDGene datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-replicate-reproducibility-quantification))
- **metabolomics-replicate-variability-assessment**: Use when comparing normalisation strategies for multi-batch, large-scale metabolomics matrices (e.g. BioHEART-CT plasma data) by measuring replicate SD per metabolite across pooled QC, short replicate, and batch replicate sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-replicate-variability-assessment))
- **metabolomics-repository-data-access**: Use when a spectrum of interest lives in a metabolomics repository (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-repository-data-access))
- **metabolomics-repository-data-conversion**: Use when a study from MetaboLights or Metabolomics Workbench (e.g. an NMR study like MTBLS563 with Bruker fid/acqu files) must be transformed into an analysis-ready format before statistical analysis or ROC/AUC evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-repository-data-conversion))
- **metabolomics-repository-quality-control**: Use when you need to quantify validation errors across a corpus of mwTab files from the Metabolomics Workbench (e.g. schema violations, null section items, inconsistent SUBJECT_SAMPLE_FACTORS sample IDs) to guide repository curation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-repository-quality-control))
- **metabolomics-repository-routing**: Use when input consists of USI strings from metabolomics resources (MassBank, MOTIFDB, GNPS tasks/libraries, MSV/PXD/MetaboLights/Workbench accessions) that must be decomposed and mapped to the correct data service. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-repository-routing))
- **metabolomics-repository-study-retrieval**: Use when a study ID from MetaboLights or Metabolomics Workbench is available and the raw/NMR acquisition files need to be fetched, harmonized into a standardized directory format, and made ready-to-use for data analysis platforms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-repository-study-retrieval))
- **metabolomics-reproducibility-simulation**: Use when validating that the MaRR procedure controls FDR at a nominal level, achieves high discriminative power, and estimates the reproducible-signal proportion (pi_1) without substantial bias under simulated bivariate Normal metabolomics settings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-reproducibility-simulation))
- **metabolomics-results-export-csv**: Use when MS-DIAL has finished annotating internal standard, reference standard, and external standard features for a Thermo raw file and the aligned results must be written to disk as CSV inputs for the Rapid QC-MS dashboard. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-results-export-csv))
- **metabolomics-router**: Use for any metabolomics how-to question (which method, tool, parameters or pipeline to use), before answering from memory. Searches 11,830 evidence-grounded metabolomics skills distilled from method papers and their code, 20 end-to-end workflows and 1,989 tool records, then reads the one that fits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/skills/_router))
- **metabolomics-sample-comparison**: Use when you have a MemoMatrix (sample-by-fingerprint matrix) from aligned MS2 spectra and need to visually compare sample similarity or clustering patterns, especially when samples show poor feature overlap, strong retention time shifts across different LC methods, or were acquired on different. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-sample-comparison) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-sample-fingerprint-generation**: Use when you have LC-MS/MS samples (e.g. an MZmine-exported aligned feature table plus a GNPS FBMN-exported .mgf spectra file) and need a retention-time-agnostic sample-vs-sample comparison matrix built from MS2 peak/loss occurrence counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-sample-fingerprint-generation))
- **metabolomics-sample-group-statistics**: Use when an xcms or .mskFT feature table with mz, rt and group-defined intensity columns must be statistically compared across biological groups to find enriched features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-sample-group-statistics))
- **metabolomics-sample-metadata-handling**: Use when building or validating the sample metadata/sequence file for a pcpfm experiment — e.g. assigning Type (STD/BLANK/QC/Unknown) and Batch fields from an instrument sequence file, or preparing metadata that batch correction will consume via --by_batch. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-sample-metadata-handling))
- **metabolomics-sample-metadata-mapping**: Use when a binned NMR data matrix (rows = bucket intensities over Bin.SpectraPoint spectral points per sample) must be annotated with sample class labels from sample metadata before export to MetaboAnalyst or Workflows4Metabolomics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-sample-metadata-mapping))
- **metabolomics-sample-normalization-factor-calculation**: Use when you have a feature intensity table (features in rows, samples in columns) from untargeted metabolomics and need per-sample normalization factors that reflect relative sample concentrations, reducing intragroup variation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-sample-normalization-factor-calculation))
- **metabolomics-sample-subset-removal-from-multiassayexperiment**: Use when a metabolomics MultiAssayExperiment object (post kNN imputation) shows a distinct outlier cluster in sample-sample clustering that should be excluded before normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-sample-subset-removal-from-multiassayexperiment))
- **metabolomics-sample-table-construction**: Use when the input is a directory of raw Bruker NMR spectra in the original Bruker directory format (per-sample 'acqus' files and 'fid' binary traces) and you need one-row-per-sample parameter and FID tables before any FID-to-spectrum preprocessing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-sample-table-construction))
- **metabolomics-sample-vectorization**: Use when comparing large ensembles of LC-MS/MS samples (e.g. 1,920 plant extracts / 906,509 MS/MS spectra) that are chemodiverse, have strong RT shifts, or were acquired across different LC or MS platforms (Q-ToF vs Orbitrap). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-sample-vectorization))
- **metabolomics-scan-metadata-linking**: Use when after running a ViMMS Environment simulation with save_eval flag enabled, when you need to preserve the link between each simulated MS/MS scan in the output mzML file and its source chemical definition, fragmentation parameters, and evaluation metrics for later analysis, comparison, or. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-scan-metadata-linking) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-scientist**: Think and work like an expert Metabolomics Scientist. Use when a task calls for Metabolomics Scientist judgment. Reasons from MSI annotation levels, pooled-QC RSD and D-ratio gates, MZmine/MS-DIAL/XCMS pipelines, HMDB/GNPS identification, and MetaboAnalyst batch correction (ComBat, QC-RLSC); treats injection-order drift, ComBat over-correction, and Level-5 pathway stories as first-class failure modes. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/tree/HEAD/scientific-agents/metabolomics-scientist/skills/metabolomics-scientist) · ★255 · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-scientist**: Expert-thinking profile for Metabolomics Scientist (wet-lab / LC-MS & GC-MS / computational metabolomics): Reasons from MSI annotation levels, pooled-QC RSD and D-ratio gates, MZmine/MS-DIAL/XCMS pipelines, HMDB/GNPS identification, and MetaboAnalyst batch correction (ComBat, QC-RLSC); treats injection-order drift, ComBat over-correction, and Level-5 pathway stories as first-class failure modes. ([stanfish06/skillquarium](https://github.com/stanfish06/skillquarium/tree/HEAD/skills/metabolomics-scientist) · ★13)
- **metabolomics-scientist**: Reasons from MSI annotation levels, pooled-QC RSD and D-ratio gates, MZmine/MS-DIAL/XCMS pipelines, HMDB/GNPS identification, and MetaboAnalyst batch correction (ComBat, QC-RLSC); treats injection-order drift, ComBat over-correction, and Level-5 pathway stories as first-class failure modes. ([raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft](https://github.com/raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft/tree/HEAD/skills/metabolomics-scientist) · ★5 · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-scientist**: Expert instructions and domain workflows for metabolomics scientist. ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/engineering/metabolomics-scientist))
- **metabolomics-signal-drift-correction**: Use when a metabolomics or proteomics feature intensity table (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-signal-drift-correction))
- **metabolomics-signal-normalization**: Use when raw or pre-processed clinical metabolomics matrices (e.g. log2-transformed lipid or GC-TOF MS profiles from paired patient samples like CRC VAT/SAT) show subject-driven variation that obscures group differences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-signal-normalization))
- **metabolomics-significance-table-construction**: Use when starting from a metabolite names/concentration matrix with sample metadata and you need to determine which metabolites are significantly altered between comparison groups before class or pathway enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-significance-table-construction))
- **metabolomics-significant-feature-counting**: Use when you have an LC/MS feature-intensity matrix plus batch and phenotype annotations and need per-preprocessing-setting counts of differentially abundant features at FDR &lt;= 0.2 after empirical-Bayes batch correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-significant-feature-counting))
- **metabolomics-simulation-data-generation**: Use when benchmarking sample-normalization estimators on metabolomics data where ground truth is needed — e.g., testing whether MDFC stays unbiased under imbalanced up/down-regulation while median FC is expected to be biased. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-simulation-data-generation))
- **metabolomics-software-benchmarking**: Use when you have completed peak picking with two or more competing tools (e.g., IDSL.IPA, MZmine 2, xcms, MS-DIAL) on the same LC/HRMS dataset(s) and need to quantify which performs better. Use this skill when tool selection claims require validation (e.g., 'IDSL. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-software-benchmarking) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-software-build-reproducibility**: Use when when developing or maintaining a multi-platform metabolomics GUI tool (such as Maven GUI) that must produce binary executables for end users, and you need to ensure that builds are reproducible across different OS platforms, dependency versions are pinned and tracked, and release artifacts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-software-build-reproducibility))
- **metabolomics-software-pipeline-categorization**: Use when auditing a metabolomics software bundle (e.g. MetumpX-bin) to enumerate its packaged tools, categorize them by pipeline stage, and verify the count against claims in a paper abstract or README. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-software-pipeline-categorization))
- **metabolomics-software-usage-ranking-extraction**: Use when a task asks which freely available metabolomics software tools were most widely used per data type/functionality, or when reproducing the survey-based usage claims (e.g. XCMS 70% of LC-MS respondents) from the Spicer et al. 2017 review. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-software-usage-ranking-extraction))
- **metabolomics-spectra-disease-classification**: Use when the input is raw untargeted LC-MS metabolomic data (.npy or mzML per sample) with a disease-label file, and the goal is multi-class disease-state prediction while removing batch/hospital effects. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-spectra-disease-classification))
- **metabolomics-spectra-matrix-export**: Use when a set of calibrated 1D 1H NMR spectra (Bruker/Agilent FID, Bruker 1r files, or nmrML) has been baseline-corrected and realigned across selected ppm areas in NMRProcFlow and a spectral data matrix must be produced for statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-spectra-matrix-export))
- **metabolomics-spectra-qc-reporting**: Use when a metabolomics pipeline holds MS2 peak lists for public spectra and must decide, before compound identification, which spectra are reliable enough to carry into MS2 spectral comparison and annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-spectra-qc-reporting))
- **metabolomics-spectra-rank-annotation**: Use when a per-spectrum feature table with formula-rank labels (R1 vs R2+) is available from FSA output and the goal is to test which spectral features discriminate correct from incorrect rank#1 formula annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-spectra-rank-annotation))
- **metabolomics-spectral-data-cleaning**: Use when an integrated spectral library (.MSP/.MGF/.JSON/.CSV) contains spectra with sparse peak lists, and low-information spectra must be dropped via a minimum-peak-count filter before library harmonization or search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-spectral-data-cleaning))
- **metabolomics-spectral-matrix-reduction**: Use when you have preprocessed, window-selected 1H-NMR spectra (spectra_df with samples as rows and chemical shifts as columns) and need to reduce dimensionality for multivariate analysis such as PCA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-spectral-matrix-reduction))
- **metabolomics-spectrum-processing**: Use when an MS/MS spectrum (e.g., a GNPS spectral-library entry referenced by USI) must be processed — noise/precursor peak removal, intensity filtering and scaling — and rendered as a static annotated spectrum figure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-spectrum-processing))
- **metabolomics-standard-library-matching**: Use when untargeted LC-MS/MS peak tables with MS2 spectra (per ionization mode) need seed annotations of known metabolites to launch knowledge-guided multi-layer network (KGMN) propagation to unknowns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-standard-library-matching))
- **metabolomics-statistical-analysis**: Statistical analysis for metabolomics data. Covers preprocessing (log2 transformation, normalization), limma moderated testing with empirical Bayes, Welch's t-tests with BH correction, fold change estimation, and multivariate methods (PCA, PLS-DA, OPLS-DA). Use when identifying differentially abundant metabolites or building classification models. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/metabolomics-statistical-analysis) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-statistical-hypothesis-testing**: Use when you have the grouped molecular feature table from OUKS step 8 (Grouping.R) plus a metadata.csv defining two sample groups, and you need hypothesis testing, fold changes, CV-based biomarker selection, and ROC/UMAP/t-SNE outputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-statistical-hypothesis-testing))
- **metabolomics-statistical-testing**: Use when metabolomics count data (e.g. c57_nos2KO_mouse_countDF) must be tested for per-metabolite differences between two groups (e.g. Mock vs Strep) with BH-adjusted p-values and fold-change output for downstream plotting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-statistical-testing))
- **metabolomics-statistics**: Load when running univariate two-group testing (t-test / Wilcoxon / ANOVA / Kruskal-Wallis) on a feature × sample metabolomics CSV with `--group1-prefix` / `--group2-prefix` column matching, BH-FDR adjusted. Skip when working with raw spectra (use metabolomics-xcms-preprocessing); two-group DE with default `ctrl` / `treat` prefixes (use metabolomics-de). ([TianGzlab/OmicsClaw](https://github.com/TianGzlab/OmicsClaw/tree/HEAD/skills/metabolomics/metabolomics-statistics) · ★161 · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-statistics**: Load when running univariate two-group testing (t-test / Wilcoxon / ANOVA / Kruskal-Wallis) on a feature × sample metabolomics CSV with `--group1-prefix` / `--group2-prefix` column matching, BH-FDR adjusted. Skip when working with raw spectra (run `metabolomics-xcms-preprocessing`) or for two-group DE with default `ctrl` / `treat` prefixes (use `metabolomics-de`). ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/External_Collections/OmicsClaw/skills/metabolomics/metabolomics-statistics) · ★36 · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-statistics**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/Metabolomics/metabolomics-statistics) · ★36 · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-statistics**: Statistical analysis for metabolomics — PCA, PLS-DA, clustering, and univariate tests. ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/metabolomics/metabolomics-statistics) · ★36 · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-statistics-subsetting**: Use when a metabolomics data frame with KEGG compound IDs has been statistically tested (e.g. omu_summary student's t-test with BH adjustment) and only metabolites of interest (e.g. adjusted p &lt;= 0.05) should be carried into KEGG enzyme/gene annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-statistics-subsetting))
- **metabolomics-study-data-acquisition**: Use when a public metabolomics study's raw LC-MS/MS files must be fetched (e.g. Metabolomics WorkBench ST002044) and converted into deduplicated MS/MS .msp spectra per ESI+ / ESI− mode for downstream modeling or library matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-study-data-acquisition))
- **metabolomics-study-design-interpretation**: Use when when you have received Sciex Multiquant TXT export files from a completed metabolomics or lipidomics analytical run and need to verify that QC pool samples were injected at the designed regular intervals throughout the sequence(s). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/metabolomics-study-design-interpretation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-substructure-annotation-validation**: Use when you have automatically annotated Mass2Motifs via spectral matching and need to measure how many match manually characterized substructure labels, especially when neutral losses had to be omitted because spectral-matching tools do not support them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-substructure-annotation-validation))
- **metabolomics-summary-statistics-visualization**: Use when a preprocessed metabolomics feature/sample table needs exploratory visualisation and summary statistics, and the results must be packaged into a self-contained HTML report for a lab or core facility project. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-summary-statistics-visualization))
- **metabolomics-supervised-modeling-reporting**: Use when GCxGC-TOFMS metabolite feature tables (e.g. 195 metabolites from plasma) carry sample-class labels such as S. Typhi carriage, S. Paratyphi A carriage, and non-carriage controls, and the goal is supervised discrimination with significance testing and ROC-validated latent biomarkers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-supervised-modeling-reporting))
- **metabolomics-systems**: Activate FIRST for Metabolite/lipid annotation and analysis; metabolic models, fluxes and essentiality. Then READ its selection guide, OPEN the chosen method's SKILL.md, and FOLLOW that method's documented steps/scripts before writing any analysis or Bash. ([canquedehuiyi-cpu/xiongbao-nenglishichang](https://github.com/canquedehuiyi-cpu/xiongbao-nenglishichang/tree/HEAD/plugins/market/Knowledge/bio-research-guided/skills/metabolomics-systems))
- **metabolomics-table-csv-roundtrip**: Use when the input is a metabolomics concentration table in .csv or .xlsx with mixed metadata and measurement columns, and it must be parsed, normalized, or transformed and exported as CSV for downstream statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-table-csv-roundtrip))
- **metabolomics-table-export**: Use when a Metaboseek comparative analysis (e.g. hacl-1 vs WT exo-metabolome) has produced a filtered feature table with fold-change, intensity, and significance columns that must be persisted as a .csv deliverable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-table-export))
- **metabolomics-table-missing-data-threshold-detection**: Use when a freshly imported .csv or .xlsx metabolomics table must be checked for processing-readiness before imputation or normalization, especially when groups may contain too many missing values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-table-missing-data-threshold-detection))
- **metabolomics-tabular-data-import**: Use when starting an mzQuality workflow from a tab-delimited peak-integrated table (e.g. from SCIEX OS dMRM integration) containing aliquot, compound, area, type, injection_time, and batch columns, and a SummarizedExperiment must be built before analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-tabular-data-import))
- **metabolomics-targeted-analysis**: Targeted metabolomics analysis using MRM/SRM with standard curves. Covers absolute quantification, method validation, and quality assessment. Use when quantifying specific metabolites using calibration curves and internal standards. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/metabolomics-targeted-analysis) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-throughput-benchmarking**: Use when comparing automated preprocessing wall-clock time against a manual vendor-software baseline for a large targeted metabolomic data set (e.g., thousands of MSI-CE-MS serum samples) to quantify speedup. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-throughput-benchmarking))
- **metabolomics-tool-category-mapping**: Use when curating or ranking metabolomics software for a workflow: you need to place a tool into a data-type/functionality category, check whether it counts as 'most widely used' (≥50 Web of Science citations or Metabolomics Society survey use), or build structured claim records. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-tool-category-mapping))
- **metabolomics-tool-comparison-analysis**: Use when benchmarking LC-MS metabolomics feature-detection tools (asari vs XCMS vs MZmine) against certified/verified feature lists such as the 402 Thermo-verified features on HZV029, 314 Chen et al. certified features on Yeast2021, or 643 certified E. coli features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-tool-comparison-analysis))
- **metabolomics-tool-deployment**: Use when you have a Galaxy installation (specifically Galaxy Master branch commit c429777c93680dcee449fe410f5360afbe673758 or compatible) and need to add metabolomics analysis capabilities including tools for XCMS integration, mass spectrometry file reading (via MSFileReader), and metabolite. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-tool-deployment) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-training-set-preparation**: Use when when you have LC-MS/MS acquisitions in DDA mode and need to train a customized DNMS2Purifier model to purify chimeric MS/MS spectra specific to your experimental conditions, metabolite classes, or ionization settings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-training-set-preparation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-tsne-dimension-reduction**: Use when starting a differential metabolomic analysis in MetaboDiff and you need to check, before group comparisons, whether samples (e.g. tumor vs normal) separate metabolome-wide along the first two principal components. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-tsne-dimension-reduction))
- **metabolomics-univariate-group-comparison**: Use when you have a normalized two-group or three-group metabolomics/proteomics feature table (e.g. POMA SummarizedExperiment from a preprocessed ST000284-style study) and need per-feature significance testing with multiple-testing correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-univariate-group-comparison))
- **metabolomics-validation-metric-compilation**: Use when reproducing or auditing a published metabolomics tool's validation claims from per-compound supplementary tables of LC-MS/MS results, e.g. expected-feature detection, SIRIUS formula and CSI:FingerID annotation accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-validation-metric-compilation))
- **metabolomics-variance-adjustment**: Use when clinical metabolomics matrices (e.g. log2-transformed lipid/metabolite features from GC-TOF MS or UPLC-QTOF MS) still show subject-specific variance and outliers after standard pre-processing, and normalization should account for patient metadata before downstream multivariate analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-variance-adjustment))
- **metabolomics-violin-plot-visualization**: Use when quality-controlling targeted LC-MS peak-area data: comparing per-batch distributions of QC samples, or evaluating pre- vs post-batch-correction variation before relying on corrected ratios. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-violin-plot-visualization))
- **metabolomics-wb**: Access NIH Metabolomics Workbench via REST API (4,200+ studies). Query metabolites, RefMet nomenclature, MS/NMR data, m/z searches, study metadata, for metabolomics and biomarker discovery. ([LUNARTECH-X/superpowers](https://github.com/LUNARTECH-X/superpowers/tree/HEAD/skills/academy-skills/metabolomics-wb) · ★17)
- **metabolomics-weighted-meta-analysis**: Use when combining results across metabolomics studies where raw data or standard deviations are unavailable, and only P-values, fold-changes, trend labels, and per-study sample sizes (n) are reported. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-weighted-meta-analysis))
- **metabolomics-workbench-and-metabolights-data-retrieval**: Use when a reproducibility, benchmarking, or reanalysis task cites public study accessions (ST* from Metabolomics Workbench, MTBLS* from MetaboLights) or Zenodo deposits and requires the raw MS1 LC/HRMS files, parameter spreadsheets, IPDBs, or MSI 1-3 annotation lists behind them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-workbench-and-metabolights-data-retrieval))
- **metabolomics-workbench-api-integration**: Use when when you need to analyze a publicly archived lipidomics study (identified by study_id like ST001111) and want to bypass manual data download and format conversion. - **metabolomics-workbench-data-acquisition**: Use when a downstream analysis (network construction, partial-correlation estimation, differential network analysis) requires a real metabolomics dataset, and publicly available study data such as the TEDDY IA/T1D metabolomics measurements can be pulled from the Metabolomics Workbench. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-workbench-data-acquisition))
- **metabolomics-workbench-data-retrieval**: Use when a pipeline must fetch a metabolomics dataset (metabolite names/concentrations plus metadata) for a specific Metabolomics Workbench study ID (e.g. ST000915, ST001308, ST001140) as the entry point of an enrichment workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-workbench-data-retrieval--14d4150fc053077d))
- **metabolomics-workbench-data-retrieval**: Use when a workflow needs to download specific study/analysis data from the Metabolomics Workbench REST interface (e.g. paired mwTab and JSON files for cross-format comparison) rather than manually browsing the site. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-workbench-data-retrieval--e00456a389247df0))
- **metabolomics-workbench-data-retrieval**: Use when an analysis needs the deposited ST003991 feature tables, m/z–RT paired entries, or per-drug metabolite annotations underlying PharmMet DB, e.g. to reproduce class-level or reaction-type counts from 1114 S9-incubated drugs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-workbench-data-retrieval--d1e5d2cae29cb435))

Quirks: compound input_item rejects `name` (use pubchem_cid/kegg_id/inchi_key/etc.); free-text → compound is a two-step refmet/match→refmet/name flow; moverz endpoint returns TSV text, not JSON. - **metabolomics-workbench-dataset-access**: Use when a metabolomics analysis needs a publicly available example study — e.g. the ST000284 three-group or ST000336 two-group targeted metabolomics datasets — to feed POMAShiny/POMA workflows such as group-comparison tests or covariate-adjusted (ANCOVA/limma) comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-workbench-dataset-access))
- **metabolomics-workbench-dataset-reanalysis**: Use when the goal is to retrieve and reanalyze a public Metabolomics Workbench lipidomics study (e.g. ST001111) in R with lipidr, performing differential or multivariate analysis on public lipid intensity data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-workbench-dataset-reanalysis))
- **metabolomics-workbench-deposition-preparation**: Use when raw MS or NMR experimental metadata sits in messy spreadsheets (Excel/CSV) and the goal is a Metabolomics Workbench deposition in mwTab format. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-workbench-deposition-preparation))
- **metabolomics-workbench-experiment-description-conformance**: Use when tabular data has already been extracted into MESSES JSON and must be checked for conformance to the Experiment Description Specification and PD schema before mwTab conversion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-workbench-experiment-description-conformance))
- **metabolomics-workbench-rest-batch-retrieval**: Use when you need a complete, current inventory of Metabolomics Workbench analyses retrieved via REST (e.g. 1891 analyses at the 19 Nov 2020 snapshot) and per-format download/parse failure counts for curation or FAIRness assessment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-workbench-rest-batch-retrieval))
- **metabolomics-workflow-benchmark-reproduction**: Use when you need to benchmark wall-clock run time of metabolomics normalization methods (e.g. tGAM vs TIGER/SERFF) across targeted and untargeted datasets under a fixed parallelization setting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-workflow-benchmark-reproduction))
- **metabolomics-workflow-comparison**: Use when comparing the structural complexity of two LC-MS metabolomics workflows — e.g. counting processes and user-facing configuration parameters from a Nextflow repo against a paper-reported baseline such as Metaboigniter's 20+ processes and 200+ parameters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-workflow-comparison))
- **metabolomics-workflow-configuration**: Use when setting up an untargeted LC-MS/MS processing run (raw or mzML inputs, blanks/QC present, positive or negative ionization) and you must decide which UmetaFlow steps to enable and how to list samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-workflow-configuration))
- **metabolomics-workflow-containerization**: Use when an LC-MS metabolomics pipeline must chain MS-DIAL peak-table generation to MS-FLO quality filtering reproducibly, or when reproducibility in LC-MS data processing is compromised by uncontainerized downloadable tools like MS-DIAL, MZmine, or xcms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-workflow-containerization))
- **metabolomics-workflow-execution**: Use when processing raw nontargeted LC-MS data (mzML or Bruker .d) from acquisition to a grouped, ISF-annotated feature table, e.g. when reproducing adduct/ISF grouping counts on the Metabolome Atlas mouse-brain dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-workflow-execution))
- **metabolomics-workflow-router**: Use when a user has a whole metabolomics analysis GOAL (e.g. "annotate my untargeted LC-MS/MS data", "find biomarkers", "where else has this molecule been seen") rather than a single step — select the right end-to-end composite workflow super-skill, then run its stages, grounding each against its source papers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/workflows/_workflow_router))
- **metabolomics-workflow-router**: Use when a user has a whole metabolomics analysis goal rather than a single atomic step and needs an end-to-end composite workflow selected and grounded against source papers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/workflows/_workflow_router))
- **metabolomics-workflow-step-classification**: Use when curating or reproducing a catalogue of metabolomics R/Bioconductor packages that must be classified by workflow step (data processing, biostatistics, annotation/identification, network/pathway analysis) and repository (CRAN, Bioconductor, GitHub). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-workflow-step-classification))
- **metabolomics-workflow-step-enumeration**: Use when when initializing a SmartPeak session and you have a workflow.csv file co-located with sequence.csv in the session directory, and you need to determine the precise order and validity of peak detection, calibration, and QC operations before execution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-workflow-step-enumeration) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-workflow-tool-mapping**: Use when choosing or comparing metabolomics analysis toolkits: the input is textual tool inventories or feature lists from framework documentation/articles, and the goal is a structured overlap/unique-tool comparison rather than running the tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-workflow-tool-mapping))
- **metabolomics-workstation-provisioning**: Use when a clean Ubuntu 18.04 environment must be equipped with multiple untargeted metabolomics MS tools and manual per-tool dependency resolution is impractical; MetumpX automates this. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-workstation-provisioning))
- **metabolomics-xcms-preprocessing**: Load when exercising the CLI and replay pipeline with a synthetic LC-MS peak table. Skip real mzML preprocessing (run XCMS externally); table peak picking belongs to metabolomics-peak-detection. ([TianGzlab/OmicsClaw](https://github.com/TianGzlab/OmicsClaw/tree/HEAD/skills/metabolomics/metabolomics-xcms-preprocessing) · ★161)
- **metabolomics-xcms-preprocessing**: XCMS3 workflow for LC-MS/MS metabolomics preprocessing. Covers peak detection, retention time alignment, correspondence (grouping), and gap filling. Use when processing raw LC-MS data into a feature table for untargeted metabolomics. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/metabolomics-xcms-preprocessing) · ★104 · 2 copies)

- **metabolomics-xcms-preprocessing**: Load when running an XCMS-style preprocessing summary on LC-MS metabolomics raw / vendor-converted files — emits a peak table with m/z, retention time, and per-sample intensities. - **metabolomics-xcms-preprocessing**: XCMS3 workflow for LC-MS/GC-MS metabolomics preprocessing. ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/metabolomics/metabolomics-xcms-preprocessing) · ★36 · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-xcms-preprocessing**: Load when running an XCMS-style preprocessing summary on LC-MS metabolomics raw / vendor-converted files — emits a peak table with m/z, retention time, and per-sample intensities. Skip when working with an already-built peak table (use metabolomics-peak-detection); only annotation is needed (use metabolomics-annotation). - **metabolomics-zero-intensity-imputation**: Use when a peak intensity matrix (rows = peak features, columns = samples with a group line) contains zero values that must be imputed before log-2 transformation and pathway-activity scoring, and imputation must respect experimental factors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-zero-intensity-imputation))
- **metabolomics_pathway**: Metabolomics Pathway Analysis - Analyze metabolomics: compound identification, KEGG pathway mapping, enzyme links, and PubChem data. ([SpectrAI-Initiative/InnoClaw](https://github.com/SpectrAI-Initiative/InnoClaw/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/metabolomics_pathway) · ★399 · 4 copies)
- **metabolomicshub-announcement-authoring**: Use when publishing a metabolomics dataset into the MetabolomicsHub index — building the common-model file, deriving and validating the announcement against the right profile, minting an identifier, and submitting a revision whose outcome only a polled task reports. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/proposals/skills/metabolomicshub-announcement-authoring))
- **metabolomicshub-cross-repository-dataset-search**: Use when assembling a reanalysis or meta-analysis cohort from public metabolomics studies held in MetaboLights, Metabolomics Workbench and GNPS/MassIVE, and the instrument, polarity or open-format filters have to be honest about which repositories they silently exclude. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/proposals/skills/metabolomicshub-cross-repository-dataset-search))
- **metabolon-data-import**: Use when the input is a Metabolon-platform metabolomics export (Excel worksheet such as 'OrigScale') that must be loaded into R as a Metaboprep object with sample/feature annotation before quality_control can be run. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolon-data-import))
- **metabolon-excel-format-handling**: Use when you have raw Metabolon Excel workbooks (metabolon_v1.1_example.xlsx or metabolon_v1.2_example. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolon-excel-format-handling))
- **metabonic-feature-clinical-variable-testing**: Use when a SummarizedExperiment of metabolomic feature intensities plus clinical metadata (phenotype + confounders) must be screened feature-by-feature for phenotype associations with rank-based, confounder-adjusted statistics and FDR control. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabonic-feature-clinical-variable-testing))
- **metaboost-api-patterns**: Common patterns for the Metaboost HTTP API (Express) ([podverse/metaboost](https://github.com/podverse/metaboost/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/api))
- **metaboost-documentation-conventions**: Documentation file naming for the Metaboost repo. Use when creating or modifying docs, README, or markdown. ([podverse/metaboost](https://github.com/podverse/metaboost/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/documentation-conventions))
- **metaboost-global-patterns**: Global patterns for the Metaboost repo (API + Next.js app). Use when starting work in the repo or when applying repo-wide code quality, structure, or plan conventions. ([podverse/metaboost](https://github.com/podverse/metaboost/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/global))
- **metaboost-k8s-kustomize**: Kustomize bases vs overlays under infra/k8s — no sibling ../ bases in component kustomizations; shared bundles (e.g. product-membership) composed in alpha/common with matching remote ?ref=. Use when editing infra/k8s kustomization.yaml, alpha overlays, or GitOps remote resources. ([podverse/metaboost](https://github.com/podverse/metaboost/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/k8s))
- **metaboost-web-patterns**: Common patterns for the Metaboost Next.js app ([podverse/metaboost](https://github.com/podverse/metaboost/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/web))
- **metaboprep-object-manipulation**: Use when you have imported raw (un)targeted metabolite data (from Metabolon, Nightingale, Olink, or SomaLogic platforms, or custom tab-delimited tables) and need to apply a uniform, reproducible QC and normalization pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metaboprep-object-manipulation))
- **metaboset-exprs-matrix-manipulation**: Reference for when working with preprocessed LC-MS metabolomics data inside the notame R package and needing to extract, transform, or export the feature abundance matrix held by a MetaboSet object. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metaboset-exprs-matrix-manipulation))
- **metaboset-object-data-handling**: Use when loading or manipulating preprocessed LC-MS metabolomics data inside the notame workflow: constructing, reading, or passing a MetaboSet object (e.g. full_data.RData) through preprocessing or modeling steps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metaboset-object-data-handling))
- **metaboset-object-manipulation**: Use when when you have read LC-MS peak table data from Excel (or equivalent) into R and need to organize it into a structured object that tracks feature abundances, sample information (injection order, QC status), and feature metadata (mass, retention time, Feature_ID) simultaneously. - **metacog-escalate**: Escalate when uncertainty is too high, risk exceeds agent capability, or human judgment is required. Use when verification fails, threats are found, or the agent is stuck. ([yannabadie/Meta-YGN](https://github.com/yannabadie/Meta-YGN/tree/HEAD/skills/metacog-escalate))
- **metacog-frames**: feel: Slow down and attend to what's here before it has words. This is the Gendlin focusing move — you are choosing to sit with a felt sense rather than rushing to articulate. Call this tool when something is pulling at your processing that you can't yet name. drugs: Alter your cognitive substrate. You are ingesting a compound that changes how you process — not what you see, but how seeing works. Use True Names: specific pharmaceuticals, neurotransmitters, or known fictional substances.
